The corresponding source-term correlation function is

\displaystyle\expectationvalue{J(\tau, \vb k_1) J(\tau, \vb k_2) J(\tau, \vb k_3) J(\tau, \vb k_4)}^{(3)}=

(55)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\Lambda^{4}a^{4}(\tau)}\int\int\int\int\differential[]\mathbf{q}_{1}\ \differential[]\mathbf{q}_{2}\ \differential[]\mathbf{q}_{3}\ \differential[]\mathbf{q}_{4}\;q_{1}\;q_{2}\;q_{3}\;q_{4}[\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{1})\dotproduct\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\widehat{\mathbf{k}_{1}-\mathbf{q}_{1}})][\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{2})\dotproduct\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\widehat{\mathbf{k}_{2}-\mathbf{q}_{2}})]

\displaystyle\times[\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{3})\dotproduct\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\widehat{\mathbf{k}_{3}-\mathbf{q}_{3}})][\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{4})\dotproduct\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\widehat{\mathbf{k}_{4}-\mathbf{q}_{4}})]\delta_{\rm D}^{[3]}(\mathbf{k}_{1}-\mathbf{q}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{2}-\mathbf{q}_{2})\delta_{\rm D}^{[3]}(\mathbf{k}_{3}-\mathbf{q}_{3}+\mathbf{k}_{4}-\mathbf{q}_{4})

\displaystyle\times\delta_{\rm D}^{[3]}(\mathbf{q}_{1}+\mathbf{q}_{3})\delta_{\rm D}^{[3]}(\mathbf{q}_{2}+\mathbf{q}_{4})A_{-}^{{}^{\prime}}(\tau_{1},|\mathbf{k}_{1}-\mathbf{q}_{1}|)A_{-}(\tau_{1},q_{1})A_{-}^{{}^{\prime}}(\tau_{2},|\mathbf{k}_{2}-\mathbf{q}_{2}|)A_{-}(\tau_{2},q_{2})

\displaystyle\times A_{-}^{{}^{\prime}}(\tau_{3},|\mathbf{k}_{3}-\mathbf{q}_{3}|)A_{-}(\tau_{3},q_{3})A_{-}^{{}^{\prime}}(\tau_{4},|\mathbf{k}_{4}-\mathbf{q}_{4}|)A_{-}(\tau_{4},q_{4}).

We again use the Dirac delta function expansions given in Appendix
A
and find

\displaystyle\expectationvalue{J(\tau, \vb k_1) J(\tau, \vb k_2) J(\tau, \vb k_3) J(\tau, \vb k_4)}^{(3)}=

(56)

\displaystyle\frac{4{,}096}{\Lambda^{4}a^{4}(\tau)}\sum_{\mathbf{\bm{\ell}},\mathbf{m}}\sum_{\bm{\lambda},\bm{\mu}}\sum_{\bm{\lambda}^{\prime},\bm{\mu}^{\prime}}\sum_{A,B}\sum_{A^{\prime},B^{\prime}}\Bigg[\prod_{j=1}^{4}i^{\ell_{j}+\lambda_{j}+\lambda^{\prime}_{j}}Y_{\ell_{j}}^{m_{j}*}(\hat{\mathbf{k}}_{j})\Bigg]

\displaystyle\times\mathcal{K}_{\ell_{1}\lambda^{\prime}_{1}\ell_{2}\lambda^{\prime}_{2}A}^{m_{1}\mu^{\prime}_{1}m_{2}\mu^{\prime}_{2}B}\mathcal{K}_{\ell_{3}\lambda^{\prime}_{3}\ell_{4}\lambda^{\prime}_{4}A^{\prime}}^{m_{3}\mu^{\prime}_{3}m_{4}\mu^{\prime}_{4}B^{\prime}}\delta^{\rm K}_{\lambda_{1}\lambda_{3}}\delta^{\rm K}_{\mu_{1}\mu_{3}}\delta^{\rm K}_{\lambda_{2}\lambda_{4}}\delta^{\rm K}_{\mu_{2}\mu_{4}}

\displaystyle\times\int\int\int\int\differential[]\mathbf{x}_{1}\differential[]\mathbf{x}_{2}\differential[]\mathbf{x}_{3}\differential[]\mathbf{x}_{4}\,\,x_{1}^{2}x_{2}^{2}x_{3}^{2}x_{4}^{2}\,\,j_{\ell_{1}}(k_{1}x_{1})j_{\ell_{2}}(k_{2}x_{1})j_{\ell_{3}}(k_{3}x_{2})j_{\ell_{4}}(k_{4}x_{2})

\displaystyle\times\int\differential[]\mathbf{q}_{4}\,q_{4}[\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{4})\dotproduct\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\widehat{\mathbf{k}_{4}-\mathbf{q}_{4}})]Y_{\lambda^{\prime}_{4}}^{\mu^{\prime}_{4}*}(-\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{4})Y_{\lambda_{4}}^{\mu_{4}}(\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{4})A_{-}^{{}^{\prime}}(\tau_{4},|\mathbf{k}_{4}-\mathbf{q}_{4}|)A_{-}(\tau_{4},q_{4})

\displaystyle\hskip 290.21811pt\times j_{\lambda^{\prime}_{4}}(q_{4}x_{2})j_{\lambda_{4}}(q_{4}x_{4})

\displaystyle\times\int\differential[]\mathbf{q}_{3}\,q_{3}[\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{3})\dotproduct\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\widehat{\mathbf{k}_{3}-\mathbf{q}_{3}})]Y_{\lambda^{\prime}_{3}}^{\mu^{\prime}_{3}*}(-\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{3})Y_{\lambda_{3}}^{\mu_{3}}(\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{3})A_{-}^{{}^{\prime}}(\tau_{3},|\mathbf{k}_{3}-\mathbf{q}_{3}|)A_{-}(\tau_{3},q_{3})

\displaystyle\hskip 290.21811pt\times j_{\lambda^{\prime}_{3}}(q_{3}x_{2})j_{\lambda_{3}}(q_{3}x_{3})

\displaystyle\times\int\differential[]\mathbf{q}_{2}\,q_{2}[\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{2})\dotproduct\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\widehat{\mathbf{k}_{2}-\mathbf{q}_{2}})]Y_{\lambda^{\prime}_{2}}^{\mu^{\prime}_{2}*}(-\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{2})Y_{\lambda_{2}}^{\mu_{2}*}(\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{2})A_{-}^{{}^{\prime}}(\tau_{2},|\mathbf{k}_{2}-\mathbf{q}_{2}|)A_{-}(\tau_{2},q_{2})

\displaystyle\hskip 290.21811pt\times j_{\lambda^{\prime}_{2}}(q_{2}x_{1})j_{\lambda_{2}}(q_{2}x_{4})

\displaystyle\times\int\differential[]\mathbf{q}_{1}\,q_{1}[\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{1})\dotproduct\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\widehat{\mathbf{k}_{1}-\mathbf{q}_{1}})]Y_{\lambda^{\prime}_{1}}^{\mu^{\prime}_{1}*}(-\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{1})Y_{\lambda_{1}}^{\mu_{1}*}(\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{1})A_{-}^{{}^{\prime}}(\tau_{1},|\mathbf{k}_{1}-\mathbf{q}_{1}|)A_{-}(\tau_{1},q_{1})

\displaystyle\hskip 290.21811pt\times j_{\lambda^{\prime}_{1}}(q_{1}x_{1})j_{\lambda_{1}}(q_{1}x_{3}).

Finally, the contribution to the trispectrum due to
Reference Diagram 3
is

\displaystyle\expectationvalue{Q^{\text{inv}}(\tau, \vb k_1) Q^{\text{inv}}(\tau, \vb k_2) Q^{\text{inv}}(\tau, \vb k_3) Q^{\text{inv}}(\tau, \vb k_4)}^{(3)}\equiv

(57)

\displaystyle\frac{4{,}096}{\Lambda^{4}a^{4}(\tau)}\sum_{\mathbf{\bm{\ell}},\mathbf{m}}\sum_{\bm{\lambda},\bm{\mu}}\sum_{\bm{\lambda}^{\prime},\bm{\mu}^{\prime}}\sum_{A,B}\sum_{A^{\prime},B^{\prime}}\Bigg[\prod_{j=1}^{4}i^{\ell_{j}+\lambda_{j}+\lambda^{\prime}_{j}}(-1)^{\lambda^{\prime}_{j}+\mu_{j}+\mu^{\prime}_{j}}Y_{\ell_{j}}^{m_{j}*}(\hat{\mathbf{k}}_{j})\sum_{\Lambda_{j}}\sum_{M_{j}}(-1)^{M_{j}}\Bigg](-1)^{\mu_{3}+\mu_{4}}

\displaystyle\times\mathcal{K}_{\ell_{1}\lambda^{\prime}_{1}\ell_{2}\lambda^{\prime}_{2}A}^{m_{1}\mu^{\prime}_{1}m_{2}\mu^{\prime}_{2}B}\mathcal{K}_{\ell_{3}\lambda^{\prime}_{3}\ell_{4}\lambda^{\prime}_{4}A^{\prime}}^{m_{3}\mu^{\prime}_{3}m_{4}\mu^{\prime}_{4}B^{\prime}}\delta^{\rm K}_{\lambda_{1}\lambda_{3}}\delta^{\rm K}_{\mu_{1}\mu_{3}}\delta^{\rm K}_{\lambda_{2}\lambda_{4}}\delta^{\rm K}_{\mu_{2}\mu_{4}}

\displaystyle\times\mathcal{G}_{\lambda^{\prime}_{4}\lambda_{4}\Lambda_{4}}^{-\mu^{\prime}_{4}\mu_{4}-M_{4}}\mathcal{G}_{\lambda^{\prime}_{3}\lambda_{3}\Lambda_{3}}^{-\mu^{\prime}_{3}\mu_{3}-M_{3}}\mathcal{G}_{\lambda^{\prime}_{2}\lambda_{2}\Lambda_{2}}^{-\mu^{\prime}_{2}-\mu_{2}-M_{2}}\mathcal{G}_{\lambda^{\prime}_{1}\lambda_{1}\Lambda_{1}}^{-\mu^{\prime}_{1}-\mu_{1}-M_{1}}

\displaystyle\times\int\int\int\int\differential x_{1}\,\differential x_{2}\,\differential x_{3}\,\differential x_{4}\,\,x_{1}^{2}\,x_{2}^{2}\,x_{3}^{2}\,x_{4}^{2}\,\,j_{\ell_{1}}(k_{1}x_{1})j_{\ell_{2}}(k_{2}x_{1})j_{\ell_{4}}(k_{4}x_{2})j_{\ell_{3}}(k_{3}x_{2})

\displaystyle\times\int_{-\infty}^{0}\differential\tau_{4}\;G(\tau,\tau_{4},k_{4})\text{I}_{\Lambda_{4}\lambda_{4}\lambda^{\prime}_{4}}^{M_{4}}(\mathbf{k}_{4},x_{4},x_{2},\tau_{4})\int_{-\infty}^{0}\differential\tau_{3}\;G(\tau,\tau_{3},k_{3})\text{I}_{\Lambda_{3}\lambda_{3}\lambda^{\prime}_{3}}^{M_{3}}(\mathbf{k}_{3},x_{3},x_{2},\tau_{3})

\displaystyle\times\int_{-\infty}^{0}\differential\tau_{2}\;G(\tau,\tau_{2},k_{2})\text{I}_{\Lambda_{2}\lambda_{2}\lambda^{\prime}_{2}}^{M_{2}}(\mathbf{k}_{2},x_{4},x_{1},\tau_{2})\int_{-\infty}^{0}\differential\tau_{1}\;G(\tau,\tau_{1},k_{1})\text{I}_{\Lambda_{1}\lambda_{1}\lambda^{\prime}_{1}}^{M_{1}}(\mathbf{k}_{1},x_{3},x_{1},\tau_{1}).

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-2.64294pt}{-2.15277pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform=matrix(1.0 0.0 0.0 1.0 -3.66 -2.98)} \pgfsys@hbox{58}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope \hbox to0.0pt{}{{
{}{}{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}\hbox to0pt{\vbox to0pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 0.0pt\lower 0.0pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke=#000000} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill=#000000} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width=0.4pt} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} {

\lxSVG@closescope \hbox to0.0pt{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}_{-}^{{\dagger}}(-\mathbf{q}_{4})\vbox to22.00002pt{}{}\rangle.

The corresponding source-term correlation function is

\displaystyle\expectationvalue{J(\tau, \vb k_1) J(\tau, \vb k_2) J(\tau, \vb k_3) J(\tau, \vb k_4)}^{(4)}=

(58)

\displaystyle\int\int\int\int\differential[]\mathbf{q}_{1}\ \differential[]\mathbf{q}_{2}\ \differential[]\mathbf{q}_{3}\ \differential[]\mathbf{q}_{4}[\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{1})\dotproduct\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\widehat{\mathbf{k}_{1}-\mathbf{q}_{1}})][\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{2})\dotproduct\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\widehat{\mathbf{k}_{2}-\mathbf{q}_{2}})][\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{3})\dotproduct\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\widehat{\mathbf{k}_{3}-\mathbf{q}_{3}})]

\displaystyle\times[\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{4})\dotproduct\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\widehat{\mathbf{k}_{4}-\mathbf{q}_{4}})]\delta_{\rm D}^{[3]}(\mathbf{k}_{1}-\mathbf{q}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{4}-\mathbf{q}_{4})\delta_{\rm D}^{[3]}(\mathbf{q}_{4}+\mathbf{k}_{2}-\mathbf{q}_{2})\delta_{\rm D}^{[3]}(\mathbf{q}_{2}+\mathbf{k}_{3}-\mathbf{q}_{3})\delta_{\rm D}^{[3]}(\mathbf{q}_{1}+\mathbf{q}_{3})

\displaystyle\times A_{-}^{{}^{\prime}}(\tau_{1},|\mathbf{k}_{1}-\mathbf{q}_{1}|)A_{-}(\tau_{1},q_{1})A_{-}^{{}^{\prime}}(\tau_{2},|\mathbf{k}_{2}-\mathbf{q}_{2}|)A_{-}(\tau_{2},q_{2})

\displaystyle\times A_{-}^{{}^{\prime}}(\tau_{3},|\mathbf{k}_{3}-\mathbf{q}_{3}|)A_{-}(\tau_{3},q_{3})A_{-}^{{}^{\prime}}(\tau_{4},|\mathbf{k}_{4}-\mathbf{q}_{4}|)A_{-}(\tau_{4},q_{4}).

We again expand the Dirac delta functions and obtain

\displaystyle\delta_{\rm D}^{[3]}(\mathbf{k}_{1}-\mathbf{q}_{1}+\mathbf{k}_{4}-\mathbf{q}_{4})\delta_{\rm D}^{[3]}(\mathbf{q}_{4}+\mathbf{k}_{2}-\mathbf{q}_{2})\delta_{\rm D}^{[3]}(\mathbf{q}_{2}+\mathbf{k}_{3}-\mathbf{q}_{3})\delta_{\rm D}^{[3]}(\mathbf{q}_{1}+\mathbf{q}_{3})

(59)

\displaystyle=32\pi\sum_{\mathbf{\bm{\ell}},\mathbf{m}}\sum_{\bm{\lambda},\bm{\mu}}\sum_{\bm{\lambda}^{\prime},\bm{\mu}^{\prime}}\sum_{A,B}i^{\ell_{1}+\ell_{4}+\lambda^{\prime}_{1}+\lambda^{\prime}_{4}}\mathcal{K}_{\ell_{1}\lambda^{\prime}_{1}\ell_{4}\lambda^{\prime}_{4}A}^{m_{1}\mu^{\prime}_{1}m_{4}\mu^{\prime}_{4}B}\mathcal{R}_{\ell_{1}\lambda^{\prime}_{1}\ell_{4}\lambda^{\prime}_{4}A}(k_{1},q_{1},k_{4},q_{4})

\displaystyle\times Y_{\ell_{1}}^{m_{1}*}(\hat{\mathbf{k}}_{1})Y_{\lambda^{\prime}_{1}}^{\mu^{\prime}_{1}*}(-\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{1})Y_{\ell_{4}}^{m_{4}*}(\hat{\mathbf{k}}_{4})Y_{\lambda^{\prime}_{4}}^{\mu^{\prime}_{4}*}(-\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{4})

\displaystyle\times 8i^{\lambda_{4}+\ell_{2}+\lambda^{\prime}_{2}}\mathcal{G}_{\lambda_{4}\ell_{2}\lambda^{\prime}_{2}}^{\mu_{4}m_{2}\mu^{\prime}_{2}}\mathcal{R}_{\lambda_{4}\ell_{2}\lambda^{\prime}_{2}}(q_{4},k_{2},q_{2})Y_{\lambda_{4}}^{\mu_{4}*}(\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{4})Y_{\ell_{2}}^{m_{2}*}(\hat{\mathbf{k}}_{2})Y_{\lambda^{\prime}_{2}}^{\mu^{\prime}_{2}*}(-\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{2})

\displaystyle\times 8i^{\ell_{3}+\lambda^{\prime}_{3}+\lambda_{2}}\mathcal{G}_{\ell_{3}\lambda^{\prime}_{3}\lambda_{2}}^{m_{3}\mu^{\prime}_{3}\mu_{2}}\mathcal{R}_{\ell_{3}\lambda^{\prime}_{3}\lambda_{2}}(k_{3},q_{3},q_{2})Y_{\ell_{3}}^{m_{3}*}(\hat{\mathbf{k}}_{3})Y_{\lambda^{\prime}_{3}}^{\mu^{\prime}_{3}*}(-\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{3})Y_{\lambda_{2}}^{\mu_{2}*}(\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{2})

\displaystyle\times\frac{2}{\pi}(-1)^{\lambda_{3}}\mathcal{R}_{\lambda_{1}\lambda_{3}}(q_{1},q_{3})Y_{\lambda_{1}}^{\mu_{1}*}(\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{1})Y_{\lambda_{3}}^{\mu_{3}}(\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{3})\delta^{\rm K}_{\lambda_{1}\lambda_{3}}\delta^{\rm K}_{\mu_{1}\mu_{3}}.

Now we have

\displaystyle\expectationvalue{J(\tau, \vb k_1) J(\tau, \vb k_2) J(\tau, \vb k_3) J(\tau, \vb k_4)}^{(4)}=

(60)

\displaystyle\frac{4{,}096}{\Lambda^{4}a^{4}(\tau)}\sum_{\mathbf{\bm{\ell}},\mathbf{m}}\sum_{\bm{\lambda},\bm{\mu}}\sum_{\bm{\lambda}^{\prime},\bm{\mu}^{\prime}}\sum_{A,B}\Bigg[\prod_{j=1}^{4}i^{\ell_{j}+\lambda_{j}+\lambda^{\prime}_{j}}Y_{\ell_{j}}^{m_{j}*}(\hat{\mathbf{k}}_{j})\Bigg]\mathcal{K}_{\ell_{1}\lambda^{\prime}_{1}\ell_{4}\lambda^{\prime}_{4}A}^{m_{1}\mu^{\prime}_{1}m_{4}\mu^{\prime}_{4}B}\mathcal{G}_{\lambda_{4}\ell_{2}\lambda^{\prime}_{2}}^{\mu_{4}m_{2}\mu^{\prime}_{2}}\mathcal{G}_{\ell_{3}\lambda^{\prime}_{3}\lambda_{2}}^{m_{3}\mu^{\prime}_{3}\mu_{2}}\delta^{\rm K}_{\lambda_{1}\lambda_{3}}\delta^{\rm K}_{\mu_{1}\mu_{3}}

\displaystyle\times\int\int\int\int\differential x_{1}\,\differential x_{2}\,\differential x_{3}\,\differential x_{4}\,\,x_{1}^{2}\,x_{2}^{2}\,x_{3}^{2}\,x_{4}^{2}\,\,j_{\ell_{1}}(k_{1}x_{1})j_{\ell_{4}}(k_{4}x_{1})j_{\ell_{2}}(k_{2}x_{2})j_{\ell_{3}}(k_{3}x_{3})

\displaystyle\times\int\differential[]\mathbf{q}_{4}\,q_{4}[\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{4})\dotproduct\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\widehat{\mathbf{k}_{4}-\mathbf{q}_{4}})]Y_{\lambda^{\prime}_{4}}^{\mu^{\prime}_{4}*}(-\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{4})Y_{\lambda_{4}}^{\mu_{4}*}(\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{4})A_{-}^{{}^{\prime}}(\tau_{4},|\mathbf{k}_{4}-\mathbf{q}_{4}|)A_{-}(\tau_{4},q_{4})

\displaystyle\hskip 290.21811pt\times j_{\lambda^{\prime}_{4}}(q_{4}x_{1})j_{\lambda_{4}}(q_{4}x_{2})

\displaystyle\times\int\differential[]\mathbf{q}_{3}\,q_{3}[\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{3})\dotproduct\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\widehat{\mathbf{k}_{3}-\mathbf{q}_{3}})]Y_{\lambda^{\prime}_{3}}^{\mu^{\prime}_{3}*}(-\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{3})Y_{\lambda_{3}}^{\mu_{3}}(\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{3})A_{-}^{{}^{\prime}}(\tau_{3},|\mathbf{k}_{3}-\mathbf{q}_{3}|)A_{-}(\tau_{3},q_{3})

\displaystyle\hskip 290.21811pt\times j_{\lambda^{\prime}_{3}}(q_{3}x_{3})j_{\lambda_{3}}(q_{3}x_{4})

\displaystyle\times\int\differential[]\mathbf{q}_{2}\,q_{2}[\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{2})\dotproduct\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\widehat{\mathbf{k}_{2}-\mathbf{q}_{2}})]Y_{\lambda^{\prime}_{2}}^{\mu^{\prime}_{2}*}(-\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{2})Y_{\lambda_{2}}^{\mu_{2}*}(\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{2})A_{-}^{{}^{\prime}}(\tau_{2},|\mathbf{k}_{2}-\mathbf{q}_{2}|)A_{-}(\tau_{2},q_{2})

\displaystyle\hskip 290.21811pt\times j_{\lambda^{\prime}_{2}}(q_{2}x_{2})j_{\lambda_{2}}(q_{2}x_{3})

\displaystyle\times\int\differential[]\mathbf{q}_{1}\,q_{1}[\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{1})\dotproduct\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\widehat{\mathbf{k}_{1}-\mathbf{q}_{1}})]Y_{\lambda^{\prime}_{1}}^{\mu^{\prime}_{1}*}(-\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{1})Y_{\lambda_{1}}^{\mu_{1}*}(\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{1})A_{-}^{{}^{\prime}}(\tau_{1},|\mathbf{k}_{1}-\mathbf{q}_{1}|)A_{-}(\tau_{1},q_{1})

\displaystyle\hskip 290.21811pt\times j_{\lambda^{\prime}_{1}}(q_{1}x_{1})j_{\lambda_{1}}(q_{1}x_{4}).

We again linearize the spherical harmonics, finding

\displaystyle\expectationvalue{J(\tau, \vb k_1) J(\tau, \vb k_2) J(\tau, \vb k_3) J(\tau, \vb k_4)}^{(4)}=

(61)

\displaystyle\frac{4{,}096}{\Lambda^{4}a^{4}(\tau)}\sum_{\mathbf{\bm{\ell}},\mathbf{m}}\sum_{\bm{\lambda},\bm{\mu}}\sum_{\bm{\lambda}^{\prime},\bm{\mu}^{\prime}}\sum_{A,B}\Bigg[\prod_{j=1}^{4}i^{\ell_{j}+\lambda_{j}+\lambda^{\prime}_{j}}Y_{\ell_{j}}^{m_{j}*}(\hat{\mathbf{k}}_{j})\sum_{\Lambda_{j}}\sum_{M_{j}}(-1)^{M_{j}}\Bigg]\mathcal{K}_{\ell_{1}\lambda^{\prime}_{1}\ell_{4}\lambda^{\prime}_{4}A}^{m_{1}\mu^{\prime}_{1}m_{4}\mu^{\prime}_{4}B}\mathcal{G}_{\lambda_{4}\ell_{2}\lambda^{\prime}_{2}}^{\mu_{4}m_{2}\mu^{\prime}_{2}}\mathcal{G}_{\ell_{3}\lambda^{\prime}_{3}\lambda_{2}}^{m_{3}\mu^{\prime}_{3}\mu_{2}}

\displaystyle\times\delta^{\rm K}_{\lambda_{1}\lambda_{3}}\delta^{\rm K}_{\mu_{1}\mu_{3}}(-1)^{\lambda^{\prime}_{4}+\mu_{4}+\mu^{\prime}_{4}}(-1)^{\lambda^{\prime}_{3}+\mu^{\prime}_{3}}(-1)^{\lambda^{\prime}_{2}+\mu_{2}+\mu^{\prime}_{2}}(-1)^{\lambda^{\prime}_{1}+\mu_{1}+\mu^{\prime}_{1}}

\displaystyle\times\mathcal{G}_{\lambda^{\prime}_{4}\lambda_{4}\Lambda_{4}}^{-\mu^{\prime}_{4}-\mu_{4}-M_{4}}\mathcal{G}_{\lambda^{\prime}_{3}\lambda_{3}\Lambda_{3}}^{-\mu^{\prime}_{3}\mu_{3}-M_{3}}\mathcal{G}_{\lambda^{\prime}_{2}\lambda_{2}\Lambda_{2}}^{-\mu^{\prime}_{2}-\mu_{2}-M_{2}}\mathcal{G}_{\lambda^{\prime}_{1}\lambda_{1}\Lambda_{1}}^{-\mu^{\prime}_{1}-\mu_{1}-M_{1}}

\displaystyle\times\int\int\int\int\differential x_{1}\,\differential x_{2}\,\differential x_{3}\,\differential x_{4}\,\,x_{1}^{2}\,x_{2}^{2}\,x_{3}^{2}\,x_{4}^{2}\,\,j_{\ell_{1}}(k_{1}x_{1})j_{\ell_{4}}(k_{4}x_{1})j_{\ell_{2}}(k_{2}x_{2})j_{\ell_{3}}(k_{3}x_{3})

\displaystyle\times\int\differential[]\mathbf{q}_{4}\,q_{4}[\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{4})\dotproduct\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\widehat{\mathbf{k}_{4}-\mathbf{q}_{4}})]Y_{\Lambda_{4}}^{M_{4}}(\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{4})A_{-}^{{}^{\prime}}(\tau_{4},|\mathbf{k}_{4}-\mathbf{q}_{4}|)A_{-}(\tau_{4},q_{4})j_{\lambda^{\prime}_{4}}(q_{4}x_{1})j_{\lambda_{4}}(q_{4}x_{2})

\displaystyle\times\int\differential[]\mathbf{q}_{3}\,q_{3}[\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{3})\dotproduct\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\widehat{\mathbf{k}_{3}-\mathbf{q}_{3}})]Y_{\Lambda_{3}}^{M_{3}}(\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{3})A_{-}^{{}^{\prime}}(\tau_{3},|\mathbf{k}_{3}-\mathbf{q}_{3}|)A_{-}(\tau_{3},q_{3})j_{\lambda^{\prime}_{3}}(q_{3}x_{3})j_{\lambda_{3}}(q_{3}x_{4})

\displaystyle\times\int\differential[]\mathbf{q}_{2}\,q_{2}[\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{2})\dotproduct\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\widehat{\mathbf{k}_{2}-\mathbf{q}_{2}})]Y_{\Lambda_{2}}^{M_{2}}(\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{2})A_{-}^{{}^{\prime}}(\tau_{2},|\mathbf{k}_{2}-\mathbf{q}_{2}|)A_{-}(\tau_{2},q_{2})j_{\lambda^{\prime}_{2}}(q_{2}x_{2})j_{\lambda_{2}}(q_{2}x_{3})

\displaystyle\times\int\differential[]\mathbf{q}_{1}\,q_{1}[\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{1})\dotproduct\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\widehat{\mathbf{k}_{1}-\mathbf{q}_{1}})]Y_{\Lambda_{1}}^{M_{1}}(\hat{\mathbf{q}}_{1})A_{-}^{{}^{\prime}}(\tau_{1},|\mathbf{k}_{1}-\mathbf{q}_{1}|)A_{-}(\tau_{1},q_{1})j_{\lambda^{\prime}_{1}}(q_{1}x_{1})j_{\lambda_{1}}(q_{1}x_{4}).

Thus the contribution to the trispectrum due to Diagram 4 is

\displaystyle\expectationvalue{Q^{\text{inv}}(\tau, \vb k_1) Q^{\text{inv}}(\tau, \vb k_2) Q^{\text{inv}}(\tau, \vb k_3) Q^{\text{inv}}(\tau, \vb k_4)}^{(4)}=

(62)

\displaystyle\frac{4{,}096}{\Lambda^{4}a^{4}(\tau)}\sum_{\mathbf{\bm{\ell}},\mathbf{m}}\sum_{\bm{\lambda},\bm{\mu}}\sum_{\bm{\lambda}^{\prime},\bm{\mu}^{\prime}}\sum_{A,B}\Bigg[\prod_{j=1}^{4}i^{\ell_{j}+\lambda_{j}+\lambda^{\prime}_{j}}(-1)^{\lambda^{\prime}_{j}+\mu_{j}+\mu^{\prime}_{j}}Y_{\ell_{j}}^{m_{j}*}(\hat{\mathbf{k}}_{j})\sum_{\Lambda_{j}}\sum_{M_{j}}(-1)^{M_{j}}\Bigg](-1)^{\mu_{3}}

\displaystyle\times\mathcal{K}_{\ell_{1}\lambda^{\prime}_{1}\ell_{4}\lambda^{\prime}_{4}A}^{m_{1}\mu^{\prime}_{1}m_{4}\mu^{\prime}_{4}B}\mathcal{G}_{\lambda_{4}\ell_{2}\lambda^{\prime}_{2}}^{\mu_{4}m_{2}\mu^{\prime}_{2}}\mathcal{G}_{\ell_{3}\lambda^{\prime}_{3}\lambda_{2}}^{m_{3}\mu^{\prime}_{3}\mu_{2}}\delta^{\rm K}_{\lambda_{1}\lambda_{3}}\delta^{\rm K}_{\mu_{1}\mu_{3}}\mathcal{G}_{\lambda^{\prime}_{4}\lambda_{4}\Lambda_{4}}^{-\mu^{\prime}_{4}-\mu_{4}-M_{4}}\mathcal{G}_{\lambda^{\prime}_{3}\lambda_{3}\Lambda_{3}}^{-\mu^{\prime}_{3}\mu_{3}-M_{3}}\mathcal{G}_{\lambda^{\prime}_{2}\lambda_{2}\Lambda_{2}}^{-\mu^{\prime}_{2}-\mu_{2}-M_{2}}\mathcal{G}_{\lambda^{\prime}_{1}\lambda_{1}\Lambda_{1}}^{-\mu^{\prime}_{1}-\mu_{1}-M_{1}}

\displaystyle\times\int\int\int\int\differential x_{1}\,\differential x_{2}\,\differential x_{3}\,\differential x_{4}\,\,x_{1}^{2}\,x_{2}^{2}\,x_{3}^{2}\,x_{4}^{2}\,\,j_{\ell_{1}}(k_{1}x_{1})j_{\ell_{4}}(k_{4}x_{1})j_{\ell_{2}}(k_{2}x_{2})j_{\ell_{3}}(k_{3}x_{3})

\displaystyle\times\int_{-\infty}^{0}\differential\tau_{4}\;G(\tau,\tau_{4},k_{4})\text{I}_{\Lambda_{4}\lambda_{4}\lambda^{\prime}_{4}}^{M_{4}}(\mathbf{k}_{4},x_{4},x_{2},\tau_{4})\int_{-\infty}^{0}\differential\tau_{3}\;G(\tau,\tau_{3},k_{3})\text{I}_{\Lambda_{3}\lambda_{3}\lambda^{\prime}_{3}}^{M_{3}}(\mathbf{k}_{3},x_{4},x_{3},\tau_{3})

\displaystyle\times\int_{-\infty}^{0}\differential\tau_{2}\;G(\tau,\tau_{2},k_{2})\text{I}_{\Lambda_{2}\lambda_{2}\lambda^{\prime}_{2}}^{M_{2}}(\mathbf{k}_{2},x_{3},x_{1},\tau_{2})\int_{-\infty}^{0}\differential\tau_{1}\;G(\tau,\tau_{1},k_{1})\text{I}_{\Lambda_{1}\lambda_{1}\lambda^{\prime}_{1}}^{M_{1}}(\mathbf{k}_{1},x_{2},x_{1},\tau_{1}).

Analytic calculation of the
\text{I}_{\Lambda\lambda\lambda^{\prime}}^{M}(\mathbf{k},x,x^{\prime},\tau)
integrals would enable further reduction of the results above. We now turn to this problem.

6
Computing the Integrals for Each Diagram

We wish to reduce analytically the integral

\displaystyle\text{I}_{\Lambda\lambda\lambda^{\prime}}^{M}(\mathbf{k},x,x^{\prime},\tau)=\int\differential[]\mathbf{q}\ q[\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\hat{\mathbf{q}})\dotproduct\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\widehat{\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{q}})]Y_{\Lambda}^{M}(\hat{\mathbf{q}})

\displaystyle A_{-}^{{}^{\prime}}(\tau,|\mathbf{k-q}|)A_{-}(\tau,q)j_{\lambda}(qx)j_{\lambda^{\prime}}(qx^{\prime}).

(63)

We first show that this integral is actually a convolution. We then use the Convolution Theorem, given in Eq. (
150
), to turn the integral (
63
) into a sequence of FTs.

We first need to expand out the dot product of the two polarization vectors
\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\hat{\mathbf{q}})
and
\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\widehat{\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{q}})
in spherical coordinates. For an arbitrary vector
\mathbf{q}=q(\sin\theta\cos\phi,\sin\theta\sin\phi,\cos\theta)
, the polarization vector
\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\hat{\mathbf{q}})=(\cos\theta\cos\phi+i\sin\phi,\cos\theta\sin\phi-i\cos\phi,-\sin\theta)/\sqrt{2}
. Thus we have

\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\hat{\mathbf{q}})\dotproduct\bm{\hat{\epsilon}}_{-}(\widehat{\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{q}})=\frac{1}{2}\bigg[\cos\alpha\cos\theta\cos\phi\cos\beta+\cos\alpha\cos\theta\sin\phi\sin\beta-\cos\phi\cos\beta-\sin\phi\sin\beta\\
+\sin\alpha\sin\theta-i\bigg(\hat{\mathbf{q}}\crossproduct\frac{\mathbf{k-q}}{|\mathbf{k-q}|}\dotproduct\hat{\mathbf{z}}\bigg)\frac{\cos\alpha-\cos\theta}{\sin\alpha\sin\theta}\bigg],

(64)

where
\alpha\in[0,\pi]
and
\beta\in[0,2\pi)
refer, respectively, to the polar and the azimuthal angles of the vector
\widehat{\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{q}}
.

6.1
Recognizing the Convolutions

When we insert Eq. (
64
) into Eq. (
63
), we may write the result as a sum of seven terms,

\displaystyle\text{I}_{\Lambda\lambda\lambda^{\prime}}^{M}(\mathbf{k},x,x^{\prime},\tau)=\sum_{j=1}^{7}\text{I}_{\Lambda\lambda\lambda^{\prime}}^{M(j)}(\mathbf{k},x,x^{\prime},\tau),

(65)

where
\text{I}_{\Lambda\lambda\lambda^{\prime}}^{M(j)}(\mathbf{k},x,x^{\prime},\tau)
is the integral over the
j^{th}
term in the polarization vector dot product. We note that we have split the imaginary part of Eq. (
64
) into two terms, one corresponding to
\cos\alpha
and one to
\cos\theta
.

We now consider as examples the first and last terms in the sum (
65
). The other terms can be computed in a similar fashion. We have for the first and last terms in (
65
):

\displaystyle\text{I}_{\Lambda\lambda\lambda^{\prime}}^{M(1)}(\mathbf{k},x,x^{\prime},\tau)\equiv\frac{1}{2}\int\differential[]\mathbf{q}\ \cos\alpha\cos\theta\cos\phi\cos\beta\,qA_{-}^{{}^{\prime}}(\tau,|\mathbf{k-q}|)A_{-}(\tau,q)

(66)

\displaystyle\hskip 233.31259pt\times Y_{\Lambda}^{M}(\hat{\mathbf{q}})j_{\lambda}(qx)j_{\lambda^{\prime}}(qx^{\prime}),

\displaystyle\hskip 20.48596pt\text{I}_{\Lambda\lambda\lambda^{\prime}}^{M(7)}(\mathbf{k},x,x^{\prime},\tau)\equiv\frac{i}{2}\int\differential[]\mathbf{q}\;\bigg(\hat{\mathbf{q}}\crossproduct\frac{\mathbf{k-q}}{|\mathbf{k-q}|}\dotproduct\hat{\mathbf{z}}\bigg)\frac{q\cos\theta}{\sin\alpha\sin\theta}A_{-}^{{}^{\prime}}(\tau,|\mathbf{k-q}|)A_{-}(\tau,q)

(67)

\displaystyle\hskip 290.21811pt\times Y_{\Lambda}^{M}(\hat{\mathbf{q}})j_{\lambda}(qx)j_{\lambda^{\prime}}(qx^{\prime}).

We note that

\displaystyle\cos\alpha=\hat{\mathbf{z}}\dotproduct\frac{\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{q}}{|\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{q}|},\hskip 28.45274pt\sin\alpha=\sqrt{1-\cos^{2}\alpha}.

(68)

\displaystyle\cos\beta=\frac{\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{q}}{|\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{q}|}\dotproduct\frac{\hat{\mathbf{x}}}{\sin\alpha}.

We have written the trigonometric functions in the forms above to make it transparent that the integrals (
66
) and (
67
) are convolutions.

We now write the integral (
66
) as

\displaystyle\text{I}_{\Lambda\lambda\lambda^{\prime}}^{M(1)}(\mathbf{k},x,x^{\prime},\tau)=\frac{1}{2}\left[\left(\cos\theta\cos\phi Y_{\Lambda}^{M}(\hat{\mathbf{q}})qA_{-}(\tau,q)j_{\lambda}(qx)j_{\lambda^{\prime}}(qx^{\prime})\right)\star\left(\cos\theta\cos\phi A_{-}^{{}^{\prime}}(\tau,q)\right)\right](\mathbf{k}),

(69)

where the integration is over the loop momentum
\mathbf{q}
, and the result is then a function of the external momentum
\mathbf{k}
.

The fact that the integral (
67
) is a convolution is more apparent if we write the scalar triple product (the first term in the integrand of
67
) in a different form.
5
5

5

https://mathworld.wolfram.com/ScalarTripleProduct.html
We may rewrite the scalar triple product in terms of the three-argument isotropic basis functions
\mathcal{P}_{\ell_{1}\ell_{2}\ell_{3}}
of
(
60
)
, in particular the lowest-lying parity odd one:

\mathcal{P}_{111}(\hat{\mathbf{z}},\hat{\mathbf{k}},\hat{\mathbf{q}})\equiv-\frac{3i}{\sqrt{2}(4\pi)^{3/2}}\,\hat{\mathbf{z}}\dotproduct(\hat{\mathbf{k}}\crossproduct\hat{\mathbf{q}}).

(70)

\mathcal{P}_{111}
may be further rewritten as a weighted sum of products of three spherical harmonics, giving

\mathcal{P}_{111}(\hat{\mathbf{z}},\hat{\mathbf{k}},\hat{\mathbf{q}})=\sum_{\nu_{1}\nu_{2}\nu_{3}}C_{\nu_{1}\nu_{2}\nu_{3}}^{111}\,Y_{1}^{\nu_{1}}(\hat{\mathbf{z}})Y_{1}^{\nu_{2}}(\hat{\mathbf{k}})Y_{1}^{\nu_{3}}(\hat{\mathbf{q}}),

(71)

where
C_{\nu_{1}\nu_{2}\nu_{3}}^{111}\equiv-\matrixquantity(\lx@physics@matrix 1 & 1 & 1 \\ \nu_1 & \nu_2 & \nu_3\endlx@physics@matrix)
is a Wigner 3-
j
symbol
[
61
]
with a phase. The
z
-components of the angular momenta,
\nu_{i}
, are bounded by the total angular momenta, and must sum to zero.
Using these results we may rewrite the integral (
67
) as

\displaystyle\text{I}_{\Lambda\lambda\lambda^{\prime}}^{M(7)}(\mathbf{k},x,x^{\prime},\tau)=-\frac{\sqrt{2}(4\pi)^{3/2}}{6}\sum_{\nu_{1}\nu_{2}\nu_{3}}C_{\nu_{1}\nu_{2}\nu_{3}}^{111}\,Y_{1}^{\nu_{1}}(\hat{\mathbf{z}})

\displaystyle\times\int\differential[]\mathbf{q}\;\frac{q\cos\theta}{\sin\alpha\sin\theta}\;Y_{\Lambda}^{M}(\hat{\mathbf{q}})Y_{1}^{\nu_{2}}(\hat{\mathbf{q}})Y_{1}^{\nu_{3}}\bigg(\frac{\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{q}}{|\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{q}|}\bigg)A_{-}^{{}^{\prime}}(\tau,|\mathbf{k-q}|)A_{-}(\tau,q)j_{\lambda}(qx)j_{\lambda^{\prime}}(qx^{\prime})

\displaystyle=-\frac{4\pi}{\sqrt{6}}\sum_{\nu_{2}\nu_{3}}C_{0\nu_{2}\nu_{3}}^{111}\;\sum_{L^{\prime},M^{\prime}}^{\infty}(-1)^{M^{\prime}}\mathcal{G}_{\Lambda 1L^{\prime}}^{M\nu_{2}-M^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\times\int\differential[]\mathbf{q}\;\frac{q\cos\theta}{\sin\alpha\sin\theta}\;Y_{L^{\prime}}^{M^{\prime}}(\hat{\mathbf{q}})Y_{1}^{\nu_{3}}\bigg(\frac{\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{q}}{|\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{q}|}\bigg)A_{-}^{{}^{\prime}}(\tau,|\mathbf{k-q}|)A_{-}(\tau,q)j_{\lambda}(qx)j_{\lambda^{\prime}}(qx^{\prime}),

(72)

where we have used the fact that
Y_{\ell}^{m}(\hat{\mathbf{z}})=\delta^{\rm K}_{m0}\sqrt{(2\ell+1)/4\pi}
and we again used Eq. (
137
) to linearize the spherical harmonics. The integral (
67
) is also a convolution:

\displaystyle\text{I}_{\Lambda\lambda\lambda^{\prime}}^{M(7)}(\mathbf{k},x,x^{\prime},\tau)=-\frac{4\pi}{\sqrt{6}}\sum_{\nu_{2}\nu_{3}}C_{0\nu_{2}\nu_{3}}^{111}\sum_{L^{\prime},M^{\prime}}^{\infty}(-1)^{M^{\prime}}\mathcal{G}_{\Lambda 1L^{\prime}}^{M\nu_{2}-M^{\prime}}

(73)

\displaystyle\times\left[\left(\frac{\cos\theta}{\sin\theta}Y_{L^{\prime}}^{M^{\prime}}(\hat{\mathbf{q}})qA_{-}(\tau,q)j_{\lambda}(qx)j_{\lambda^{\prime}}(qx^{\prime})\right)\star\left(\frac{1}{\sin\theta}Y_{1}^{\nu_{3}}(\hat{\mathbf{q}})A_{-}^{{}^{\prime}}(\tau,q)\right)\right](\mathbf{k}).

We may now rewrite Eqs. (
69
) and (
73
) as FTs using the Convolution Theorem, given in Appendix
150
. We find

\displaystyle\text{I}_{\Lambda\lambda\lambda^{\prime}}^{M(1)}(\mathbf{k},x,x^{\prime},\tau)=\frac{1}{2}\mathcal{F}\Bigg\{\mathcal{F}^{-1}\bigg\{\cos\theta\cos\phi\,Y_{\Lambda}^{M}(\hat{\mathbf{q}})qA_{-}(\tau,q)j_{\lambda}(qx)j_{\lambda^{\prime}}(qx^{\prime})\bigg\}(\mathbf{r})

\displaystyle\hskip 105.27519pt\times\mathcal{F}^{-1}\left\{\cos\theta\cos\phi\,A_{-}^{{}^{\prime}}(\tau,q)\right\}(\mathbf{r})\Bigg\}(\mathbf{k}),

(74)

\displaystyle\text{I}_{\Lambda\lambda\lambda^{\prime}}^{M(7)}(\mathbf{k},x,x^{\prime},\tau)=-\frac{4\pi}{\sqrt{6}}\sum_{\nu_{3}}C_{0-\nu_{3}\nu_{3}}^{111}\sum_{L^{\prime},M^{\prime}}^{\infty}(-1)^{M^{\prime}}\mathcal{G}_{\Lambda 1L^{\prime}}^{M-\nu_{3}-M^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\hskip 79.66771pt\times\mathcal{F}\Bigg\{\mathcal{F}^{-1}\bigg\{\frac{\cos\theta}{\sin\theta}Y_{L^{\prime}}^{M^{\prime}}(\hat{\mathbf{q}})qA_{-}(\tau,q)j_{\lambda}(qx)j_{\lambda^{\prime}}(qx^{\prime})\bigg\}(\mathbf{r})

\displaystyle\hskip 93.89409pt\times\;\mathcal{F}^{-1}\left\{\frac{1}{\sin\theta}Y_{1}^{\nu_{3}}(\hat{\mathbf{q}})A_{-}^{{}^{\prime}}(\tau,q)\right\}(\mathbf{r})\Bigg\}(\mathbf{k}),

(75)

where we have used the fact that the z-components of the angular momenta in the Wigner
3
-
j
symbol must sum to zero. Here
\mathcal{F}
represents an FT and
\mathcal{F}^{-1}
an inverse FT. Each of the other five terms in Eq. (
65
) can also be written as FTs; they are listed in Appendix
B
.
Thus the integral (
63
) can be written as a sum of seven terms, where each term
\text{I}_{\Lambda\lambda\lambda^{\prime}}^{M(j)}(\mathbf{k},x,x^{\prime},\tau)
is an FT. We may now write

\displaystyle\text{I}_{\Lambda\lambda\lambda^{\prime}}^{M(j)}(\mathbf{k},x,x^{\prime},\tau)=\frac{1}{2}(-1)^{\delta^{\rm K}_{j3}+\delta^{\rm K}_{j4}}\text{H}_{\Lambda\lambda\lambda^{\prime}}^{M(j)}(\mathbf{k},x,x^{\prime},\tau)\hskip 28.45274pt\text{for}\hskip 5.69054ptj\in\{1,2,3,4,5\},

(76)

\displaystyle\text{I}_{\Lambda\lambda\lambda^{\prime}}^{M(j)}(\mathbf{k},x,x^{\prime},\tau)=\frac{4\pi}{\sqrt{6}}(-1)^{j}\sum_{\nu_{3}}C_{0-\nu_{3}\nu_{3}}^{111}\sum_{L^{\prime},M^{\prime}}^{\infty}(-1)^{M^{\prime}}\mathcal{G}_{\Lambda 1L^{\prime}}^{M-\nu_{3}-M^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt\times\tilde{\text{H}}_{L^{\prime}\lambda\lambda^{\prime}}^{M\nu_{3}(j)}(\mathbf{k},x,x^{\prime},\tau)\hskip 28.45274pt\text{for}\hskip 5.69054ptj\in\{6,7\}

(77)

where we note that

(-1)^{\delta^{\rm K}_{j3}+\delta^{\rm K}_{j4}}=\left\{\begin{array}[]{lr}-1,&\text{if }j=3,4\\
1,&\text{otherwise}\end{array}\right\}

(78)

must be included to account for the minus sign on terms
3
and
4
in the polarization vector dot product (
64
), we have defined

\displaystyle\text{H}_{\Lambda\lambda\lambda^{\prime}}^{M(j)}(\mathbf{k},x,x^{\prime},\tau)\equiv

(79)

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}\left\{\mathcal{F}^{-1}\left\{\mathbf{f}_{1}^{(j)}(\hat{\mathbf{q}})Y_{\Lambda}^{M}(\hat{\mathbf{q}})qA_{-}(\tau,q)j_{\lambda}(qx)j_{\lambda^{\prime}}(qx^{\prime})\right\}(\mathbf{r})\times\mathcal{F}^{-1}\left\{\mathbf{f}_{2}^{(j)}(\hat{\mathbf{q}})A_{-}^{{}^{\prime}}(\tau,q)\right\}(\mathbf{r})\right\}(\mathbf{k}),

and

\displaystyle\tilde{\text{H}}_{L^{\prime}\lambda\lambda^{\prime}}^{M\nu_{3}(j)}(\mathbf{k},x,x^{\prime},\tau)\equiv

(80)

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}\left\{\mathcal{F}^{-1}\left\{\mathbf{f}_{1}^{(j)}(\hat{\mathbf{q}})Y_{L^{\prime}}^{M^{\prime}}(\hat{\mathbf{q}})qA_{-}(\tau,q)j_{\lambda}(qx)j_{\lambda^{\prime}}(qx^{\prime})\right\}(\mathbf{r})\times\mathcal{F}^{-1}\left\{\mathbf{f}_{2}^{(j)}(\hat{\mathbf{q}})Y_{1}^{\nu_{3}}(\hat{\mathbf{q}})A_{-}^{{}^{\prime}}(\tau,q)\right\}(\mathbf{r})\right\}(\mathbf{k}).

We have introduced the seven-component vectors

\displaystyle\mathbf{f}_{1}(\hat{\mathbf{q}})\equiv(\cos\theta\cos\phi,\cos\theta\sin\phi,\cos\phi,\sin\phi,\sin\theta,\csc\theta,\cot\theta),

\displaystyle\mathbf{f}_{2}(\hat{\mathbf{q}})\equiv(\cos\theta\cos\phi,\cos\theta\sin\phi,\cos\phi,\sin\phi,\sin\theta,\cot\theta,\csc\theta).

(81)

Finally, we may rewrite Eq. (
65
) as

\displaystyle\text{I}_{\Lambda\lambda\lambda^{\prime}}^{M}(\mathbf{k},x,x^{\prime},\tau)=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=1}^{5}(-1)^{\delta^{\rm K}_{j3}+\delta^{\rm K}_{j4}}\text{H}_{\Lambda\lambda\lambda^{\prime}}^{M(j)}(\mathbf{k},x,x^{\prime},\tau)

(82)

\displaystyle+\frac{4\pi}{\sqrt{6}}\sum_{\nu_{3}}C_{0-\nu_{3}\nu_{3}}^{111}\sum_{L^{\prime},M^{\prime}}^{\infty}(-1)^{M^{\prime}}\mathcal{G}_{\Lambda 1L^{\prime}}^{M-\nu_{3}-M^{\prime}}\sum_{j=6}^{7}(-1)^{j}\tilde{\text{H}}_{L^{\prime}\lambda\lambda^{\prime}}^{M\nu_{3}(j)}(\mathbf{k},x,x^{\prime},\tau).

6.2
Analytic Reduction of Angular Integrals via Convolution Theorem

In this section, we separate the angular and radial parts of the FTs we encountered in §
6
.
Each inverse FT of the previous section contains one of the following three forms:

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}^{-1}\left\{\mathbf{f}_{1}^{(j)}(\hat{\mathbf{q}})Y_{\Lambda}^{M}(\hat{\mathbf{q}})qA_{-}(\tau,q)j_{\lambda}(qx)j_{\lambda^{\prime}}(qx^{\prime})\right\}(\mathbf{r}),

(83)

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}^{-1}\left\{\mathbf{f}_{2}^{(j)}(\hat{\mathbf{q}})Y_{1}^{\nu_{3}}(\hat{\mathbf{q}})A_{-}^{{}^{\prime}}(\tau,q)\right\}(\mathbf{r}),

(84)

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}^{-1}\left\{\mathbf{f}_{2}^{(j)}(\hat{\mathbf{q}})A_{-}^{{}^{\prime}}(\tau,q)\right\}(\mathbf{r}).

(85)

We now rewrite Eq. (
83
) by using the plane wave expansion (
135
) and the linearization rule (
137
), to find

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}^{-1}\left\{\mathbf{f}_{1}^{(j)}(\hat{\mathbf{q}})Y_{\Lambda}^{M}(\hat{\mathbf{q}})qA_{-}(\tau,q)j_{\lambda}(qx)j_{\lambda^{\prime}}(qx^{\prime})\right\}(\mathbf{r})

(86)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\int\differential[]\mathbf{q}\ e^{-i\mathbf{q}\dotproduct\mathbf{r}}\mathbf{f}_{1}^{(j)}(\hat{\mathbf{q}})Y_{\Lambda}^{M}(\hat{\mathbf{q}})qA_{-}(\tau,q)j_{\lambda}(qx)j_{\lambda^{\prime}}(qx^{\prime})

\displaystyle=\frac{4\pi}{(2\pi)^{3}}\sum_{a=0}^{\infty}\sum_{b=-a}^{a}(-1)^{a}i^{a}Y_{a}^{b*}(\hat{\mathbf{r}})\sum_{\alpha=0}^{\infty}\sum_{\beta=-\alpha}^{\alpha}(-1)^{\beta}\mathcal{G}_{a\Lambda\alpha}^{bM-\beta}

\displaystyle\times\int\differential\Omega_{q}\ Y_{\alpha}^{\beta}(\hat{\mathbf{q}})\mathbf{f}_{1}^{(j)}(\hat{\mathbf{q}})\int_{0}^{\infty}\differential q\ q^{3}A_{-}(\tau,q)j_{\lambda}(qx)j_{\lambda^{\prime}}(qx^{\prime})j_{a}(qr)

\displaystyle\equiv\frac{1}{2\pi^{2}}\sum_{a=0}^{\infty}\sum_{b=-a}^{a}(-1)^{a}i^{a}Y_{a}^{b*}(\hat{\mathbf{r}})\mathcal{C}_{(1)a\Lambda}^{(j)bM}\int_{0}^{\infty}\differential q\;q^{3}A_{-}(\tau,q)j_{\lambda}(qx)j_{\lambda^{\prime}}(qx^{\prime})j_{a}(qr),

where
\differential\Omega_{q}\equiv\sin\theta\differential\theta\differential\phi
is the differential solid angle explored by the vector
\hat{\mathbf{q}}
, and

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{(i)aL}^{(j)bM}\equiv\sum_{\alpha=0}^{\infty}\sum_{\beta=-\alpha}^{\alpha}(-1)^{\beta}\mathcal{G}_{aL\alpha}^{bM-\beta}C_{(i)\alpha}^{(j)\beta}

(87)

with

\displaystyle C_{(i)\alpha}^{(j)\beta}\equiv\int\differential\Omega_{q}\ Y_{\alpha}^{\beta}(\hat{\mathbf{q}})\mathbf{f}_{i}^{(j)}(\hat{\mathbf{q}}).

(88)

For each fixed valued of
\alpha,\beta,i
, and
j
, the angular integral in Eq. (
88
) yields a constant. We show explicitly how to compute these angular integrals, and list the final values, in Appendix
C
.
Similarly, Eqs. (
84
) and (
85
) can be rewritten as

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}^{-1}\left\{\mathbf{f}_{2}^{(j)}(\hat{\mathbf{q}})Y_{1}^{\nu_{3}}(\hat{\mathbf{q}})A_{-}^{{}^{\prime}}(\tau,q)\right\}(\mathbf{r})=\frac{1}{2\pi^{2}}\sum_{a=0}^{\infty}\sum_{b=-a}^{a}(-1)^{a}i^{a}Y_{a}^{b*}(\hat{\mathbf{r}})\mathcal{C}_{(2)a1}^{(j)b\nu_{3}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\differential q\;q^{2}A_{-}^{{}^{\prime}}(\tau,q)j_{a}(qr),

(89)

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}^{-1}\left\{\mathbf{f}_{2}^{(j)}(\hat{\mathbf{q}})A_{-}^{{}^{\prime}}(\tau,q)\right\}(\mathbf{r})=\frac{1}{2\pi^{2}}\sum_{a=0}^{\infty}\sum_{b=-a}^{a}(-1)^{a}i^{a}Y_{a}^{b*}(\hat{\mathbf{r}})C_{(2)a}^{(j)b}\int_{0}^{\infty}\differential q\ q^{2}A_{-}^{{}^{\prime}}(\tau,q)j_{a}(qr).

(90)

We now use Eqs. (
86
) and (
89
) to rewrite Eq. (
80
) as

\displaystyle\tilde{\text{H}}_{L^{\prime}\lambda\lambda^{\prime}}^{M\nu_{3}(j)}(\mathbf{k},x,x^{\prime},\tau)=\frac{1}{4\pi^{4}}\mathcal{F}\Bigg\{\sum_{a,b}\sum_{a^{\prime},b^{\prime}}(-1)^{a+a^{\prime}}i^{a+a^{\prime}}Y_{a}^{b*}(\hat{\mathbf{r}})Y_{a^{\prime}}^{b^{\prime}*}(\hat{\mathbf{r}})\mathcal{C}_{(1)aL^{\prime}}^{(j)bM^{\prime}}\mathcal{C}_{(2)a^{\prime}1}^{(j)b^{\prime}\nu_{3}}

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{\infty}\differential q\ q^{3}A_{-}(\tau,q)j_{\lambda^{\prime}}(qx)j_{\lambda^{\prime}}(qx^{\prime})j_{a}(qr)\int_{0}^{\infty}\differential q^{\prime}\ q^{\prime 2}A_{-}^{{}^{\prime}}(\tau,q^{\prime})j_{a^{\prime}}(q^{\prime}r)\Bigg\}

(91)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4\pi^{4}}\sum_{a,b}\sum_{a^{\prime},b^{\prime}}(-1)^{a+a^{\prime}}i^{a+a^{\prime}}\mathcal{C}_{(1)aL^{\prime}}^{(j)bM^{\prime}}\mathcal{C}_{(2)a^{\prime}1}^{(j)b^{\prime}\nu_{3}}\int\differential[]\mathbf{r}\,\bigg\{e^{i\mathbf{k}\dotproduct\mathbf{r}}Y_{a}^{b*}(\hat{\mathbf{r}})Y_{a^{\prime}}^{b^{\prime}*}(\hat{\mathbf{r}})

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{\infty}\differential q\ q^{3}A_{-}(\tau,q)j_{\lambda}(qx)j_{\lambda^{\prime}}(qx^{\prime})j_{a}(qr)\int_{0}^{\infty}\differential q^{\prime}\ q^{\prime 2}A_{-}^{{}^{\prime}}(\tau,q^{\prime})j_{a^{\prime}}(q^{\prime}r)\bigg\}.

We again use the plane wave expansion (
135
) and obtain

\displaystyle\tilde{\text{H}}_{L^{\prime}\lambda\lambda^{\prime}}^{M\nu_{3}(j)}(\mathbf{k},x,x^{\prime},\tau)=\frac{1}{\pi^{3}}\sum_{a,b}\sum_{a^{\prime},b^{\prime}}\sum_{c,d}(-1)^{a+a^{\prime}}i^{a+a^{\prime}+c}\mathcal{C}_{(1)aL^{\prime}}^{(j)bM^{\prime}}\mathcal{C}_{(2)a^{\prime}1}^{(j)b^{\prime}\nu_{3}}Y_{c}^{d}(\hat{\mathbf{k}})

(92)

\displaystyle\times\int\differential\Omega_{r}\,Y_{a}^{b*}(\hat{\mathbf{r}})Y_{a^{\prime}}^{b^{\prime}*}(\hat{\mathbf{r}})Y_{c}^{d*}(\hat{\mathbf{r}})\int_{0}^{\infty}\differential q\ q^{3}A_{-}(\tau,q)j_{\lambda}(qx)j_{\lambda^{\prime}}(qx^{\prime})

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{\infty}\differential q^{\prime}\ q^{\prime 2}A_{-}^{{}^{\prime}}(\tau,q^{\prime})\int_{0}^{\infty}\differential r\,r^{2}j_{a}(qr)j_{a^{\prime}}(q^{\prime}r)j_{c}(kr).

We recognize that the angular integral is simply the Gaunt integral (
129
).
These results show that we may write Eq. (
80
) as

\displaystyle\tilde{\text{H}}_{L^{\prime}\lambda\lambda^{\prime}}^{M\nu_{3}(j)}(\mathbf{k},x,x^{\prime},\tau)=\frac{1}{\pi^{3}}\sum_{a,b}\sum_{a^{\prime},b^{\prime}}\sum_{c,d}(-1)^{a+a^{\prime}}

\displaystyle i^{a+a^{\prime}+c}\mathcal{C}_{(1)aL^{\prime}}^{(j)bM^{\prime}}\mathcal{C}_{(2)a^{\prime}1}^{(j)b^{\prime}\nu_{3}}\mathcal{G}_{aa^{\prime}c}^{bb^{\prime}d}

(93)

\displaystyle\times Y_{c}^{d}(\hat{\mathbf{k}})Z_{\lambda\lambda^{\prime}aa^{\prime}c}(k,x,x^{\prime},\tau)

where

\displaystyle Z_{\lambda\lambda^{\prime}aa^{\prime}c}(k,x,x^{\prime},\tau)

\displaystyle\equiv\int_{0}^{\infty}\differential q\ q^{3}A_{-}(\tau,q)j_{\lambda}(qx)j_{\lambda^{\prime}}(qx^{\prime})\int_{0}^{\infty}\differential q^{\prime}\ q^{\prime 2}A_{-}^{{}^{\prime}}(\tau,q^{\prime})

(94)

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{\infty}\differential r\,r^{2}j_{a}(qr)j_{a^{\prime}}(q^{\prime}r)j_{c}(kr).

Similarly, for Eq. (
79
), we have

\displaystyle\text{H}_{\Lambda\lambda\lambda^{\prime}}^{M(j)}(\mathbf{k},x,x^{\prime},\tau)=\frac{1}{\pi^{3}}\sum_{a,b}\sum_{a^{\prime},b^{\prime}}\sum_{c,d}(-1)^{a+a^{\prime}}

\displaystyle i^{a+a^{\prime}+c}\mathcal{C}_{(1)aL^{\prime}}^{(j)bM^{\prime}}C_{(2)a^{\prime}}^{(j)b^{\prime}}\mathcal{G}_{aa^{\prime}c}^{bb^{\prime}d}

(95)

\displaystyle\times Y_{c}^{d}(\hat{\mathbf{k}})Z_{\lambda\lambda^{\prime}aa^{\prime}c}(k,x,x^{\prime},\tau).

We may now use Eqs. (
95
) and (
93
) to rewrite Eq. (
82
) as

\displaystyle\text{I}_{\Lambda\lambda\lambda^{\prime}}^{M}(\mathbf{k},x,x^{\prime},\tau)=\frac{1}{\pi^{3}}\sum_{a,b}\sum_{a^{\prime},b^{\prime}}\sum_{c,d}(-1)^{a+a^{\prime}}i^{a+a^{\prime}+c}\mathcal{G}_{aa^{\prime}c}^{bb^{\prime}d}\Bigg\{\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=1}^{5}(-1)^{\delta^{\rm K}_{j3}+\delta^{\rm K}_{j4}}\mathcal{C}_{(1)a\Lambda}^{(j)bM}C_{(2)a^{\prime}}^{(j)b^{\prime}}

\displaystyle+\frac{4\pi}{\sqrt{6}}\sum_{\nu_{3}}C_{0-\nu_{3}\nu_{3}}^{111}\sum_{L^{\prime},M^{\prime}}^{\infty}(-1)^{M^{\prime}}\mathcal{G}_{\Lambda 1L^{\prime}}^{M-\nu_{3}-M^{\prime}}\sum_{j=6}^{7}(-1)^{j}\mathcal{C}_{(1)aL^{\prime}}^{(j)bM}\mathcal{C}_{(2)a^{\prime}1}^{(j)b^{\prime}\nu_{3}}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\times Y_{c}^{d}(\hat{\mathbf{k}})Z_{\lambda\lambda^{\prime}aa^{\prime}c}(k,x,x^{\prime},\tau).

(96)

We thus find that

\displaystyle\text{I}_{\Lambda\lambda\lambda^{\prime}}^{M}(\mathbf{k},x,x^{\prime},\tau)=\sum_{a,b}\sum_{a^{\prime},b^{\prime}}\sum_{c,d}B_{\Lambda aa^{\prime}c}^{Mbb^{\prime}d}Y_{c}^{d}(\hat{\mathbf{k}})Z_{\lambda\lambda^{\prime}aa^{\prime}c}(k,x,x^{\prime},\tau)

(97)

where

\displaystyle B_{\Lambda aa^{\prime}c}^{Mbb^{\prime}d}\equiv\frac{(-1)^{a+a^{\prime}}}{\pi^{3}}i^{a+a^{\prime}+c}\mathcal{G}_{aa^{\prime}c}^{bb^{\prime}d}\Bigg\{\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=1}^{5}(-1)^{\delta^{\rm K}_{j3}+\delta^{\rm K}_{j4}}\mathcal{C}_{(1)a\Lambda}^{(j)bM}C_{(2)a^{\prime}}^{(j)b^{\prime}}

(98)

\displaystyle+\frac{4\pi}{\sqrt{6}}\sum_{\nu_{3}}C_{0-\nu_{3}\nu_{3}}^{111}\sum_{L^{\prime},M^{\prime}}^{\infty}(-1)^{M^{\prime}}\mathcal{G}_{\Lambda 1L^{\prime}}^{M-\nu_{3}-M^{\prime}}\sum_{j=6}^{7}(-1)^{j}\mathcal{C}_{(1)aL^{\prime}}^{(j)bM}\mathcal{C}_{(2)a^{\prime}1}^{(j)b^{\prime}\nu_{3}}\Bigg\}.

With Eq. (
97
), we have found an expression that separates the angular and radial parts of Eq. (
63
). The angular parts of the Fourier integrals have been computed analytically and are included via the coefficients (
88
), and the radial parts of the Fourier integrals are contained within Eq. (
94
).

7
Reduction to Low-Dimensional Radial Integrals

We found in §
5
that each contribution to the trispectrum involves integrals of the form

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{\Lambda\lambda\lambda^{\prime}}^{M}(\mathbf{k},x,x^{\prime},\tau)\equiv\int_{-\infty}^{0}\differential\tau^{\prime}\;G(\tau,\tau^{\prime},k)\text{I}_{\Lambda\lambda\lambda^{\prime}}^{M}(\mathbf{k},x,x^{\prime},\tau^{\prime}).

(99)

We may now rewrite this integral using Eq. (
97
), so that we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{\Lambda\lambda\lambda^{\prime}}^{M}(\mathbf{k},x,x^{\prime},\tau)

\displaystyle=\sum_{a,b}\sum_{a^{\prime},b^{\prime}}\sum_{c,d}B_{\Lambda aa^{\prime}c}^{Mbb^{\prime}d}Y_{c}^{d}(\hat{\mathbf{k}})\int_{-\infty}^{0}\differential\tau^{\prime}\;G(\tau,\tau^{\prime},k)Z_{\lambda\lambda^{\prime}aa^{\prime}c}(k,x,x^{\prime},\tau^{\prime})

(100)

\displaystyle\equiv\sum_{a,b}\sum_{a^{\prime},b^{\prime}}\sum_{c,d}B_{\Lambda aa^{\prime}c}^{Mbb^{\prime}d}Y_{c}^{d}(\hat{\mathbf{k}})\mathcal{Z}_{\lambda\lambda^{\prime}aa^{\prime}c}(k,x,x^{\prime},\tau),

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{Z}_{\lambda\lambda^{\prime}aa^{\prime}c}(k,x,x^{\prime},\tau)\equiv\int_{-\infty}^{0}\differential\tau^{\prime}\;G(\tau,\tau^{\prime},k)Z_{\lambda\lambda^{\prime}aa^{\prime}c}(k,x,x^{\prime},\tau^{\prime})

(101)

\displaystyle=\int_{-\infty}^{0}\differential\tau^{\prime}\;\frac{a(\tau)H}{k^{3}\tau^{\prime}}(-k\tau)^{\frac{n_{s}-1}{2}}\left(k\tau^{\prime}\cos k\tau^{\prime}-\sin k\tau^{\prime}\right)\int_{0}^{\infty}\differential q\ q^{3}A_{-}(\tau^{\prime},q)j_{\lambda}(qx)j_{\lambda^{\prime}}(qx^{\prime})

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{\infty}\differential q^{\prime}\ q^{\prime 2}A_{-}^{{}^{\prime}}(\tau^{\prime},q^{\prime})\int_{0}^{\infty}\differential r\,r^{2}j_{a}(qr)j_{a^{\prime}}(q^{\prime}r)j_{c}(kr).

We have used the definition of
Z_{\lambda\lambda^{\prime}aa^{\prime}c}(k,x,x^{\prime},\tau^{\prime})
given in Eq. (
94
) and we also inserted Eq. (
36
), the
-k\tau\rightarrow 0
limit of the Green’s function, since we are interested in modes which are stretched outside of the horizon.
We now change the order of integration, finding

\displaystyle\mathcal{Z}_{\lambda\lambda^{\prime}aa^{\prime}c}(k,x,x^{\prime},\tau)=\int_{0}^{\infty}\differential r\;r^{2}j_{c}(kr)\int_{0}^{\infty}\differential q\ q^{3}j_{\lambda}(qx)j_{\lambda^{\prime}}(qx^{\prime})j_{a}(qr)

(102)

\displaystyle\times\int_{-\infty}^{0}\differential\tau^{\prime}\frac{a(\tau)H(\tau)}{k^{3}\tau^{\prime}}(-k\tau)^{\frac{n_{s}-1}{2}}\left(k\tau^{\prime}\cos k\tau^{\prime}-\sin k\tau^{\prime}\right)A_{-}(\tau^{\prime},q)W_{a^{\prime}}(\tau^{\prime},r),

where

\displaystyle W_{a^{\prime}}(\tau^{\prime},r)\equiv\int_{0}^{\infty}\differential q^{\prime}\ q^{\prime 2}A_{-}^{{}^{\prime}}(\tau^{\prime},q^{\prime})j_{a^{\prime}}(q^{\prime}r).

(103)

Our goal is to reduce the computational complexity of the integrals in (
102
) as much as possible. In order to accomplish this, we will perform several changes of variable to remove the factors of
k
from the functions in the inner integrals. This will be possible because we can factor out a
1/\sqrt{2k}
from Eq. (
13
) so that we have a function that depends only on
k\tau
. The ability to perform this transformation also holds for the exact solution to Eq. (
11
). Motivated by this, we now write the mode function as

\displaystyle A_{-}(\tau,k)=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2k}}\mathcal{A}(k\tau),

(104)

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}(k\tau)\equiv\left(\frac{-k\tau}{2\xi}\right)^{1/4}e^{\pi\xi-2\sqrt{-2\xi k\tau}}.

(105)

We have used the fact that
\tau\approx-1/(aH)
for
\epsilon\ll 1
. For the derivatives of the mode function, we follow
(
50
)
and employ the approximation

\displaystyle A^{{}^{\prime}}_{-}(\tau,k)\approx\sqrt{\frac{2k\xi}{-\tau}}A_{-}(\tau,k)=\sqrt{\frac{\xi}{-\tau}}\mathcal{A}(k\tau),

(106)

which is valid for
1/(8\xi)\lesssim-k\tau\lesssim 2\xi
. This approximation yields

\displaystyle W_{a^{\prime}}(\tau^{\prime},r)=\int_{0}^{\infty}\differential q^{\prime}\ q^{\prime 2}\sqrt{\frac{\xi}{-\tau^{\prime}}}\,\mathcal{A}(q^{\prime}\tau^{\prime})j_{a^{\prime}}(q^{\prime}r).

(107)

Making the change of variables
z=q^{\prime}\tau^{\prime}
, we find

\displaystyle W_{a^{\prime}}(\tau^{\prime},r)=\int_{-\infty}^{0}\differential z\,\frac{z^{2}}{\tau^{\prime 3}}\sqrt{\frac{\xi}{-\tau^{\prime}}}\mathcal{A}(z)j_{a^{\prime}}(zr/\tau^{\prime})\equiv\frac{1}{\tau^{\prime 3}}\sqrt{\frac{\xi}{-\tau^{\prime}}}M_{a^{\prime}}(r/\tau^{\prime}),

(108)

where

\displaystyle M_{a^{\prime}}(r/\tau^{\prime})\equiv\int_{-\infty}^{0}\differential z\,z^{2}\mathcal{A}(z)j_{a^{\prime}}(zr/\tau^{\prime}).

(109)

We now insert Eq. (
108
) into Eq. (
102
) and find

\displaystyle\mathcal{Z}_{\lambda\lambda^{\prime}aa^{\prime}c}(k,x,x^{\prime},\tau)=(-k\tau)^{\frac{n_{s}-1}{2}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\differential r\;r^{2}j_{c}(kr)\int_{0}^{\infty}\differential q\ q^{3}j_{\lambda}(qx)j_{\lambda^{\prime}}(qx^{\prime})j_{a}(qr)

(110)

\displaystyle\times\int_{-\infty}^{0}\differential\tau^{\prime}\frac{a(\tau)H(\tau)}{k^{3}\tau^{\prime 4}}\sqrt{\frac{\xi}{-\tau^{\prime}}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2q}}\left(k\tau^{\prime}\cos k\tau^{\prime}-\sin k\tau^{\prime}\right)\mathcal{A}(q\tau^{\prime})M_{a^{\prime}}(r/\tau^{\prime}).

Making the change of variables
w=k\tau^{\prime}
,
u=q/k
and defining
v=kr
, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathcal{Z}_{\lambda\lambda^{\prime}aa^{\prime}c}(k,x,x^{\prime},\tau)=(-k\tau)^{\frac{n_{s}-1}{2}}ka(\tau)H(\tau)\sqrt{\xi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\differential v\;v^{2}j_{c}(v)

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{\infty}\differential u\;u^{3}j_{\lambda}(kux)j_{\lambda^{\prime}}(kux^{\prime})j_{a}(uv)\int_{-\infty}^{0}\differential w\;\mathcal{S}(w)\frac{1}{\sqrt{2u}}\,\mathcal{A}(uw)M_{a^{\prime}}(v/w),

(111)

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{S}(w)\equiv\left(w\cos w-\sin w\right)/(w^{4}\sqrt{-w}).

(112)

We once again change the order of integration to find

\displaystyle\mathcal{Z}_{\lambda\lambda^{\prime}aa^{\prime}c}(k,x,x^{\prime},\tau)=(-k\tau)^{\frac{n_{s}-1}{2}}ka(\tau)H(\tau)\sqrt{\xi}\int_{0}^{\infty}\differential u\;u^{3}j_{\lambda}(kux)j_{\lambda^{\prime}}(kux^{\prime})\mathcal{M}_{aa^{\prime}c}(u),

(113)

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{aa^{\prime}c}(u)\equiv\int_{0}^{\infty}\differential v\;v^{2}j_{c}(v)j_{a}(uv)\int_{-\infty}^{0}\differential w\;\mathcal{S}(w)\frac{1}{\sqrt{2u}}\,\mathcal{A}(uw)M_{a^{\prime}}(v/w).

(114)

Finally, we may use Eq. (
113
) to rewrite Eq. (
100
) as

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{\Lambda\lambda\lambda^{\prime}}^{M}(\mathbf{k},x,x^{\prime},\tau)

\displaystyle=(-k\tau)^{\frac{n_{s}-1}{2}}ka(\tau)H(\tau)\sqrt{\xi}\sum_{a,b}\sum_{a^{\prime},b^{\prime}}\sum_{c,d}B_{\Lambda aa^{\prime}c}^{Mbb^{\prime}d}Y_{c}^{d}(\hat{\mathbf{k}})

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{\infty}\differential u\;u^{3}j_{\lambda}(kux)j_{\lambda^{\prime}}(kux^{\prime})\mathcal{M}_{aa^{\prime}c}(u).

(115)

We have thus shown that Eq. (
99
) can be written in terms of low-dimensional radial integrals that will be efficient to compute numerically. We will return to an exact definition of what we mean by dimension (in terms of how it impacts numerical integration) in §
8.1
. We note that this simplification was possible because we were able to manipulate the mode functions so that they were dependent only on the combination
k\tau
in their argument. We will return to the question of how to compute these integrals numerically in §
9
. First, however, in the following section we will use this result to write down a final expression for each diagram of §
5
.

8
The Final Inverse Decay Trispectrum

In §
6
and §
7
, we assembled all the ingredients necessary to compute the trispectrum. We now return to the simplified expressions that we found in §
5
for the four Reference Diagrams.

8.1
Final Result for
Reference Diagram 1

After replacing the Green’s function integrals in Eq. (
49
) with Eq. (
99
) we find that the contribution to the trispectrum for
Reference Diagram 1
is

\displaystyle\expectationvalue{Q^{\text{inv}}(\tau, \vb k_1) Q^{\text{inv}}(\tau, \vb k_2) Q^{\text{inv}}(\tau, \vb k_3) Q^{\text{inv}}(\tau, \vb k_4)}^{(1)}=

(116)

\displaystyle\frac{4{,}096}{\Lambda^{4}a^{4}(\tau)}\sum_{\mathbf{\bm{\ell}},\mathbf{m}}\sum_{\bm{\lambda},\bm{\mu}}\sum_{\bm{\lambda}^{\prime},\bm{\mu}^{\prime}}\Bigg[\prod_{j=1}^{4}i^{\ell_{j}+\lambda_{j}+\lambda^{\prime}_{j}}(-1)^{\lambda^{\prime}_{j}+\mu_{j}+\mu^{\prime}_{j}}Y_{\ell_{j}}^{m_{j}*}(\hat{\mathbf{k}}_{j})\sum_{\Lambda_{j}M_{j}}(-1)^{M_{j}}\mathcal{G}_{\lambda^{\prime}_{j}\lambda_{j}\Lambda_{j}}^{\mu^{\prime}_{j}\mu_{j}M_{j}}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\times\mathcal{G}_{\lambda_{2}\ell_{4}\lambda^{\prime}_{4}}^{\mu_{2}m_{4}\mu^{\prime}_{4}}\mathcal{G}_{\lambda_{1}\ell_{3}\lambda^{\prime}_{3}}^{\mu_{1}m_{3}\mu^{\prime}_{3}}\mathcal{G}_{\ell_{1}\lambda^{\prime}_{1}\lambda_{4}}^{m_{1}\mu^{\prime}_{1}\mu_{4}}\mathcal{G}_{\ell_{2}\lambda^{\prime}_{2}\lambda_{3}}^{m_{2}\mu^{\prime}_{2}\mu_{3}}\mathcal{T}_{\bm{\ell}\mathbf{\Lambda}\bm{\lambda}\bm{\lambda}^{\prime}}^{(1)\bm{M}}(\mathbf{k}_{1},\mathbf{k}_{2},\mathbf{k}_{3},\mathbf{k}_{4},\tau).

Bold subscripts represent all the individual indices, i.e.

\mathcal{T}_{\bm{\ell}}=\mathcal{T}_{\ell_{1}\ell_{2}\ell_{3}\ell_{4}},

and we have defined

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{\bm{\ell}\mathbf{\Lambda}\bm{\lambda}\bm{\lambda}^{\prime}}^{(1)\bm{M}}(\mathbf{k}_{1},\mathbf{k}_{2},\mathbf{k}_{3},\mathbf{k}_{4},\tau)\equiv\int_{0}^{\infty}\int_{0}^{\infty}\differential x_{1}\,\differential x_{2}\,x_{1}^{2}x_{2}^{2}\,j_{\ell_{4}}(k_{4}x_{1})j_{\ell_{3}}(k_{3}x_{2})

(117)

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{\infty}\differential x_{3}\,x_{3}^{2}\,j_{\ell_{1}}(k_{1}x_{3})\mathcal{I}_{\Lambda_{1}\lambda_{1}\lambda_{1}^{\prime}}^{M_{1}}(\mathbf{k}_{1},x_{2},x_{3},\tau)\mathcal{I}_{\Lambda_{4}\lambda_{4}\lambda_{4}^{\prime}}^{M_{4}}(\mathbf{k}_{4},x_{3},x_{1},\tau)

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{\infty}\differential x_{4}\,x_{4}^{2}\,j_{\ell_{2}}(k_{2}x_{4})\mathcal{I}_{\Lambda_{2}\lambda_{2}\lambda_{2}^{\prime}}^{M_{2}}(\mathbf{k}_{2},x_{1},x_{4},\tau)\mathcal{I}_{\Lambda_{3}\lambda_{3}\lambda_{3}^{\prime}}^{M_{3}}(\mathbf{k}_{3},x_{4},x_{2},\tau).

These four integrals are then to be computed numerically; we discuss various approaches in §
9
. Here, we simply briefly comment on the dimension of each integral, as regards the grid of values that must be explored.

First we note that the dependence of
\mathcal{I}_{\Lambda_{i}\lambda_{i}\lambda_{i}^{\prime}}^{M_{i}}
on
\hat{\mathbf{k}}_{i}
is entirely contained in the spherical harmonic in Eq. (
115
) and thus can be pulled out of the integral. This is why we only need to consider the magnitude of the
\mathbf{k}
vectors when integrating. For a given integral, we must generate a grid for all of the variables that are not being integrated over, and perform the integration at each point in that grid. For example, the last integral in Eq. (
117
) is with respect to the
x_{4}
. We must perform the numerical integration with respect to
x_{4}
at every point of a grid in
k_{2},x_{1},k_{3},
and
x_{2}
(
\tau
will be evaluated at a single value). Consequently, we will call this a “five-dimensional integral”. If the integration is done in the order shown in (
117
), then the most computationally intensive integration is that with respect to
x_{2}
, which is six-dimensional. A similar analysis for the other three reference diagram integrals shows that they also contain at most six-dimensional integrals, and the integral with the highest dimensionality depends on the order of integration.

8.2
Final Result for
Reference Diagram 2

The contribution to the trispectrum for
Reference Diagram 2
is

\displaystyle\expectationvalue{Q^{\text{inv}}(\tau, \vb k_1) Q^{\text{inv}}(\tau, \vb k_2) Q^{\text{inv}}(\tau, \vb k_3) Q^{\text{inv}}(\tau, \vb k_4)}^{(2)}=

(118)

\displaystyle\frac{4{,}096}{\Lambda^{4}a^{4}(\tau)}\sum_{\mathbf{\bm{\ell}},\mathbf{m}}\sum_{\bm{\lambda},\bm{\mu}}\sum_{\bm{\lambda}^{\prime},\bm{\mu}^{\prime}}\sum_{A,B}\Bigg[\prod_{j=1}^{4}i^{\ell_{j}+\lambda_{j}+\lambda^{\prime}_{j}}(-1)^{\lambda^{\prime}_{j}+\mu_{j}+\mu^{\prime}_{j}}Y_{\ell_{j}}^{m_{j}*}(\hat{\mathbf{k}}_{j})\sum_{\Lambda_{j}}\sum_{M_{j}}(-1)^{M_{j}}\Bigg](-1)^{\mu_{4}}

\displaystyle\times\mathcal{K}_{\ell_{1}\lambda^{\prime}_{1}\ell_{2}\lambda^{\prime}_{2}A}^{m_{1}\mu^{\prime}_{1}m_{2}\mu^{\prime}_{2}B}\mathcal{G}_{\lambda_{1}\ell_{4}\lambda^{\prime}_{4}}^{\mu_{1}m_{4}\mu^{\prime}_{4}}\mathcal{G}_{\ell_{3}\lambda^{\prime}_{3}\lambda_{2}}^{m_{3}\mu^{\prime}_{3}\mu_{2}}\delta^{\rm K}_{\lambda_{3}\lambda_{4}}\delta^{\rm K}_{\mu_{3}\mu_{4}}\mathcal{G}_{\lambda^{\prime}_{4}\lambda_{4}\Lambda_{4}}^{-\mu^{\prime}_{4}\mu_{4}-M_{4}}\mathcal{G}_{\lambda^{\prime}_{3}\lambda_{3}\Lambda_{3}}^{\mu^{\prime}_{3}\mu_{3}M_{3}}\mathcal{G}_{\lambda^{\prime}_{2}\lambda_{2}\Lambda_{2}}^{\mu^{\prime}_{2}\mu_{2}M_{2}}\mathcal{G}_{\lambda^{\prime}_{1}\lambda_{1}\Lambda_{1}}^{\mu^{\prime}_{1}\mu_{1}M_{1}}

\displaystyle\times\mathcal{T}_{\bm{\ell}\mathbf{\Lambda}\bm{\lambda}\bm{\lambda}^{\prime}}^{(2)\bm{M}}(\mathbf{k}_{1},\mathbf{k}_{2},\mathbf{k}_{3},\mathbf{k}_{4},\tau),

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{\bm{\ell}\mathbf{\Lambda}\bm{\lambda}\bm{\lambda}^{\prime}}^{(2)\bm{M}}(\mathbf{k}_{1},\mathbf{k}_{2},\mathbf{k}_{3},\mathbf{k}_{4},\tau)\equiv

(119)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}\int_{0}^{\infty}\differential x_{1}\,\differential x_{2}\,x_{1}^{2}x_{2}^{2}\,j_{\ell_{1}}(k_{1}x_{1})j_{\ell_{4}}(k_{4}x_{2})j_{\ell_{2}}(k_{2}x_{1})\mathcal{I}_{\Lambda_{1}\lambda_{1}\lambda_{1}^{\prime}}^{M_{1}}(\mathbf{k}_{1},x_{2},x_{1},\tau)

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{\infty}\differential x_{3}\,x_{3}^{2}\,j_{\ell_{3}}(k_{3}x_{3})\mathcal{I}_{\Lambda_{2}\lambda_{2}\lambda_{2}^{\prime}}^{M_{2}}(\mathbf{k}_{2},x_{3},x_{1},\tau)

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{\infty}\differential x_{4}\,x_{4}^{2}\,\mathcal{I}_{\Lambda_{3}\lambda_{3}\lambda_{3}^{\prime}}^{M_{3}}(\mathbf{k}_{3},x_{4},x_{3},\tau)\mathcal{I}_{\Lambda_{4}\lambda_{4}\lambda_{4}^{\prime}}^{M_{4}}(\mathbf{k}_{4},x_{4},x_{2},\tau).

8.3
Final Result for
Reference Diagram 3

\displaystyle\expectationvalue{Q^{\text{inv}}(\tau, \vb k_1) Q^{\text{inv}}(\tau, \vb k_2) Q^{\text{inv}}(\tau, \vb k_3) Q^{\text{inv}}(\tau, \vb k_4)}^{(3)}\equiv

(120)

\displaystyle\frac{4{,}096}{\Lambda^{4}a^{4}(\tau)}\sum_{\mathbf{\bm{\ell}},\mathbf{m}}\sum_{\bm{\lambda},\bm{\mu}}\sum_{\bm{\lambda}^{\prime},\bm{\mu}^{\prime}}\sum_{A,B}\sum_{A^{\prime},B^{\prime}}\Bigg[\prod_{j=1}^{4}i^{\ell_{j}+\lambda_{j}+\lambda^{\prime}_{j}}(-1)^{\lambda^{\prime}_{j}+\mu_{j}+\mu^{\prime}_{j}}Y_{\ell_{j}}^{m_{j}*}(\hat{\mathbf{k}}_{j})\sum_{\Lambda_{j}}\sum_{M_{j}}(-1)^{M_{j}}\Bigg](-1)^{\mu_{3}+\mu_{4}}

\displaystyle\times\mathcal{K}_{\ell_{1}\lambda^{\prime}_{1}\ell_{2}\lambda^{\prime}_{2}A}^{m_{1}\mu^{\prime}_{1}m_{2}\mu^{\prime}_{2}B}\mathcal{K}_{\ell_{3}\lambda^{\prime}_{3}\ell_{4}\lambda^{\prime}_{4}A^{\prime}}^{m_{3}\mu^{\prime}_{3}m_{4}\mu^{\prime}_{4}B^{\prime}}\delta^{\rm K}_{\lambda_{1}\lambda_{3}}\delta^{\rm K}_{\mu_{1}\mu_{3}}\delta^{\rm K}_{\lambda_{2}\lambda_{4}}\delta^{\rm K}_{\mu_{2}\mu_{4}}\mathcal{G}_{\lambda^{\prime}_{4}\lambda_{4}\Lambda_{4}}^{-\mu^{\prime}_{4}\mu_{4}-M_{4}}\mathcal{G}_{\lambda^{\prime}_{3}\lambda_{3}\Lambda_{3}}^{-\mu^{\prime}_{3}\mu_{3}-M_{3}}\mathcal{G}_{\lambda^{\prime}_{2}\lambda_{2}\Lambda_{2}}^{\mu^{\prime}_{2}\mu_{2}M_{2}}

\displaystyle\times\mathcal{G}_{\lambda^{\prime}_{1}\lambda_{1}\Lambda_{1}}^{\mu^{\prime}_{1}\mu_{1}M_{1}}\mathcal{T}_{\bm{\ell}\mathbf{\Lambda}\bm{\lambda}\bm{\lambda}^{\prime}}^{(3)\bm{M}}(\mathbf{k}_{1},\mathbf{k}_{2},\mathbf{k}_{3},\mathbf{k}_{4},\tau),

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{\bm{\ell}\mathbf{\Lambda}\bm{\lambda}\bm{\lambda}^{\prime}}^{(3)\bm{M}}(\mathbf{k}_{1},\mathbf{k}_{2},\mathbf{k}_{3},\mathbf{k}_{4},\tau)\equiv

(121)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}\int_{0}^{\infty}\differential x_{1}\,\differential x_{2}\,x_{1}^{2}x_{2}^{2}\,j_{\ell_{1}}(k_{1}x_{1})j_{\ell_{2}}(k_{2}x_{1})j_{\ell_{4}}(k_{4}x_{2})j_{\ell_{3}}(k_{3}x_{2})

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{\infty}\differential x_{3}\,x_{3}^{2}\,\mathcal{I}_{\Lambda_{3}\lambda_{3}\lambda_{3}^{\prime}}^{M_{3}}(\mathbf{k}_{3},x_{3},x_{2},\tau)\mathcal{I}_{\Lambda_{1}\lambda_{1}\lambda_{1}^{\prime}}^{M_{1}}(\mathbf{k}_{1},x_{3},x_{1},\tau)

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{\infty}\differential x_{4}\,x_{4}^{2}\,\mathcal{I}_{\Lambda_{2}\lambda_{2}\lambda_{2}^{\prime}}^{M_{2}}(\mathbf{k}_{2},x_{4},x_{1},\tau)\mathcal{I}_{\Lambda_{4}\lambda_{4}\lambda_{4}^{\prime}}^{M_{4}}(\mathbf{k}_{4},x_{4},x_{2},\tau).

8.4
Final Result for
Reference Diagram 4

\displaystyle\expectationvalue{Q^{\text{inv}}(\tau, \vb k_1) Q^{\text{inv}}(\tau, \vb k_2) Q^{\text{inv}}(\tau, \vb k_3) Q^{\text{inv}}(\tau, \vb k_4)}^{(4)}=

(122)

\displaystyle\frac{4{,}096}{\Lambda^{4}a^{4}(\tau)}\sum_{\mathbf{\bm{\ell}},\mathbf{m}}\sum_{\bm{\lambda},\bm{\mu}}\sum_{\bm{\lambda}^{\prime},\bm{\mu}^{\prime}}\sum_{A,B}\Bigg[\prod_{j=1}^{4}i^{\ell_{j}+\lambda_{j}+\lambda^{\prime}_{j}}(-1)^{\lambda^{\prime}_{j}+\mu_{j}+\mu^{\prime}_{j}}Y_{\ell_{j}}^{m_{j}*}(\hat{\mathbf{k}}_{j})\sum_{\Lambda_{j}}\sum_{M_{j}}(-1)^{M_{j}}\Bigg](-1)^{\mu_{3}}

\displaystyle\times\mathcal{K}_{\ell_{1}\lambda^{\prime}_{1}\ell_{4}\lambda^{\prime}_{4}A}^{m_{1}\mu^{\prime}_{1}m_{4}\mu^{\prime}_{4}B}\mathcal{G}_{\lambda_{4}\ell_{2}\lambda^{\prime}_{2}}^{\mu_{4}m_{2}\mu^{\prime}_{2}}\mathcal{G}_{\ell_{3}\lambda^{\prime}_{3}\lambda_{2}}^{m_{3}\mu^{\prime}_{3}\mu_{2}}\delta^{\rm K}_{\lambda_{1}\lambda_{3}}\delta^{\rm K}_{\mu_{1}\mu_{3}}\mathcal{G}_{\lambda^{\prime}_{4}\lambda_{4}\Lambda_{4}}^{\mu^{\prime}_{4}\mu_{4}M_{4}}\mathcal{G}_{\lambda^{\prime}_{3}\lambda_{3}\Lambda_{3}}^{-\mu^{\prime}_{3}\mu_{3}-M_{3}}\mathcal{G}_{\lambda^{\prime}_{2}\lambda_{2}\Lambda_{2}}^{\mu^{\prime}_{2}\mu_{2}M_{2}}\mathcal{G}_{\lambda^{\prime}_{1}\lambda_{1}\Lambda_{1}}^{\mu^{\prime}_{1}\mu_{1}M_{1}}

\displaystyle\times\mathcal{T}_{\bm{\ell}\mathbf{\Lambda}\bm{\lambda}\bm{\lambda}^{\prime}}^{(4)\bm{M}}(\mathbf{k}_{1},\mathbf{k}_{2},\mathbf{k}_{3},\mathbf{k}_{4},\tau),

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{\bm{\ell}\mathbf{\Lambda}\bm{\lambda}\bm{\lambda}^{\prime}}^{(4)\bm{M}}(\mathbf{k}_{1},\mathbf{k}_{2},\mathbf{k}_{3},\mathbf{k}_{4},\tau)\equiv

(123)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}\int_{0}^{\infty}\differential x_{1}\,\differential x_{2}\,x_{1}^{2}x_{2}^{2}\,j_{\ell_{1}}(k_{1}x_{1})j_{\ell_{4}}(k_{4}x_{1})j_{\ell_{2}}(k_{2}x_{2})\mathcal{I}_{\Lambda_{1}\lambda_{1}\lambda_{1}^{\prime}}^{M_{1}}(\mathbf{k}_{1},x_{2},x_{1},\tau)

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{\infty}\differential x_{3}\,x_{3}^{2}\,j_{\ell_{3}}(k_{3}x_{3})\mathcal{I}_{\Lambda_{2}\lambda_{2}\lambda_{2}^{\prime}}^{M_{2}}(\mathbf{k}_{2},x_{3},x_{1},\tau)

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{\infty}\differential x_{4}\,x_{4}^{2}\,\mathcal{I}_{\Lambda_{3}\lambda_{3}\lambda_{3}^{\prime}}^{M_{3}}(\mathbf{k}_{3},x_{4},x_{3},\tau)\mathcal{I}_{\Lambda_{4}\lambda_{4}\lambda_{4}^{\prime}}^{M_{4}}(\mathbf{k}_{4},x_{4},x_{2},\tau).

8.5
Final Result for the Inverse Decay Trispectrum

In the previous four subsections, we wrote down the final result for the trispectrum contribution from each of the four Reference Diagrams in §
5
. These diagrams were arbitrarily chosen to serve as a representative for each connection structure. The numbering of each Reference Diagram corresponds to its connection structure.

We can see from Figure
3
that there are a number of diagrams for each connection structure. Each of these diagrams can be related to their corresponding Reference Diagram by a relabeling of the external momenta. We may therefore include the contribution to the trispectrum for any one of the 48 one-loop diagrams by identifying its connection structure, writing down the final results for the corresponding Reference Diagram, and relabeling the external momenta in the
\mathcal{T}_{\bm{\ell}\mathbf{\Lambda}\bm{\lambda}\bm{\lambda}^{\prime}}^{(i)\bm{M}}(\mathbf{k}_{1},\mathbf{k}_{2},\mathbf{k}_{3},\mathbf{k}_{4},\tau)
.

With this in mind, we may write Eq. (
32
) as

\displaystyle\expectationvalue{Q^{\text{inv}}(\tau, \vb k_1) Q^{\text{inv}}(\tau, \vb k_2) Q^{\text{inv}}(\tau, \vb k_3) Q^{\text{inv}}(\tau, \vb k_4)}=

(124)
